# PiSXMe public recovery manifest

This checkout was assembled locally because the original iCloud-backed working tree contained File Provider dataless placeholders. It is a preservation/publication snapshot, not a claim that Rev A is fabrication-ready.

- Source path: `/Users/Cooper/Documents/ChatGPT/sxm2`
- Snapshot time (local): 2026-08-29 12:15:34 -0600
- Readable files copied: 28
- Exact recovered files substituted: 7
- Dataless files omitted: 3138
- Copy errors: 0

## Exact source snapshots

- `pisxme/PiSXMe.kicad_sch` and `.kicad_pcb` are byte-identical to the last verified active hashes recorded in the handoff work.
- `pisxme/PiSXMe.kicad_sym`, project tables, and project metadata were recovered from local disposable snapshots.

## Omitted iCloud placeholders

The following paths were present in the source tree but not locally materialized by iCloud at publication time. They were not fabricated or silently replaced. The list is exhaustive for this staging pass.

The bridge implementation files (`server.py`, `bridge/`, and related tests) are among these unavailable placeholders; the repository therefore records the limitation explicitly instead of publishing empty files.
